NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
Monday, November 10, 2014 
 
PRESENT: Jason E. Whiting, Supervisor; Jonathan M. Nez, Supervisor; Dawnafe Whitesinger, Supervisor; 
and Jesse Thompson, Chairman were present. David Tenney, Supervisor was absent. 
9:06 a.m.  Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation led by Chairman Thompson. 
CALL TO THE PUBLIC: Terry Hill, representing VFW Post 9907, thanked the Board of Supervisors for their 
support of the Veteran’s and their issues.  He reported there would be several Veterans’ programs this week which 
includes the Nicholas Homestead School program in Show Low. He stated that on November 11 there would be 
a Veteran’s Day Ceremony at 11:00 a.m. at the Show Low Veterans Memorial. Mr. Hill dis cussed other events 
for Veterans on Wednesday, including the Blue Ridge School  which has a Veteran’s program and  a Veterans 
program at the Vernon School in Apache County.  Mr. Hill expressed thanks to the communities for their support 
of the veterans and the VFW Post 9907. Steven Slaton spoke regarding the Voter Rights Act and the early voting 
sites on the reservations. He expressed his concern t hat this issue was not put on the Board’s agenda. Joyce Hill 
expressed her concern that the issue of Voter Outreach on the Reservation had not been put on the agenda as 
requested, at the  last Board meeting. Mrs. Hill asked that the Board look into this iss ue and r eport back to the  
public. Karen MacKean addressed the Board regarding the Early V oting Outreach sites on the reservation. She 
indicated that it was unconstitutional and must be cleared by the Federal Court. She spoke of the voting right act 
and asked that this issue be put on the agenda for the next BOS meeting on November 25th. Chairman Thompson 
stated their concerns were  acknowledged. Chairman Thompson indicated the purpose of the Early Voting on 
the reservation is to get more people invol ved in the political process. He stated there will be a discussion with 
staff.  
      CONSENT AGENDA: a) Minutes: October 28, 2014 b) Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000 October 2014 
c) Clerk of Superior Court October 2014 d) Juvenile Probation Report October 2014 e) Justice Court Reports: 
Holbrook Precinct #1, October 2014; Winslow Precinct #2, September 2014; Snowflake Precinct #3, October 
2014; Kayenta Precinct #4, October 2014; Show Low Precinct #5, September 2014; and Pinetop -Lakeside #6, 
October 2014 f) Constable Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1, August 2014 and Snowflake Precinct #3, September 
2014 g) 15 th Amendment to revolving credit agreement for newly created Fire District Timber Mesa Fire and 
Medical District h) Request for undercover plates i) Letter of support to National Community Health Partners : 
Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to authorize the Chairman to sign the items in the consent agenda as 
presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  
a. Presentation acknowledging the service and commitment of Navajo County employees that are Veterans 
of the United States armed forces : Chairman Thompson acknowledged all of the veterans and all of 
those serving in the arm ed forces, who are protecting our freedoms in this  country. A presentation was 
played of pictures of our employees and their family members that have served or are currently serving. 
Supervisor Whiting, Supervisor Nez, Supervisor Whitesinger and James Jayne, County Manager made 
comments and expressed their appreciation to all of the veterans. Chairman Thompson read a poem and 
gave all veterans present in the audience a Navajo County lapel pin and expressed his appreciation to the 
Veterans.

<!-- page 2 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 11-10-2014 
    Page 2 of 4 
 
b. Presentation of the Miss Navajo County Scholarship Pageant Fund: Rachele Baldwin addressed the Board 
regarding the Miss Navajo County Scholarship Pageant Fund : Abrianna Swanson, Miss Navajo County 
2015, Emily Eavenson, Miss Navajo County Outstanding Teen 2015 & Aspynne Seymore, M iss White 
Mountain Outstanding Teen 2015 a ddressed the Board members regarding their platforms. Supervisor 
Whiting provided the program with funding support and thanked them for their service to Navajo County.  
c. Consideration and possible approval of the Bo ard of Supervisors meeting schedule for 2015 : Melissa 
Buckley, Clerk of the Board  addressed the Board regarding the 2015 Board of Supervisors meeting 
schedule. Supervisor Nez made a motion to approve the 2015 Board of Supervisors meeting schedule; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
d. Consideration and possible acceptance of the back tax land list and authorization to publish the notice of 
the same scheduling the 2014 Back Tax Land On -Line Auction to begin on November 24, 2014 at 9:00  
a.m.: Melissa Buckley, Clerk of the Board addressed the Board regarding the back tax land auction. She 
indicated that the on line auction has increased property sales. Supervisor Nez made a motion to approve 
the back tax land list and authorization to publish the notice of the same scheduling the 2014 Back Tax 
Land On-Line Auction to begin on November 24, 2014 at 9:00 a.m. ; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Mr. Jayne thanked Melissa for her efforts. 
      HUMAN RESOURCES: Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions : Kimberly Eavenson 
reviewed the 18 item personnel action report. Supervisor Nez made a motion to approve the personnel actions 
as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
NAVAJO COUNTY PUBLIC HEALTH SERVICES DISTRICT: Board of Directors Session : Supervisor 
Nez made a motion to enter into the Navajo County Public Health Services District Board of Directors Session; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
a. CONSENT AGENDA:  
i) Intergovernmental Rental Agreement between Navajo County Public Health Services District and 
Kayenta Unified School District effective July 1, 2014 to June 30, 2015 in the amount of $774.20 per 
month: 
ii) Professional Services Contract with Mauriah Walker, R.N. for Public Health Nurse services to fill 
contract requirements for Immunization, Family Planning, TB, STD and HIV grants with the Arizona 
Department of Health Services at the rate of $35.00 per hour:  Mary Herring indicated the two consent 
items are of a continuing nature.  Supervisor Nez made a motion  to approve the consent agenda as 
presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
b. Consideration and possible approval of a Professional Services Contract with Rebecca Sowers, R.N. for 
Public Health Nurse Services to fill contract requirement for Immunization, Family Planning, TB, STD 
and HIV grants with the Arizona Department of Health Services at th e rate of $35.00 per hour : Mary 
Herring discussed the item. Supervisor Nez made a motion to approve the Professional Services Contract 
with Rebecca Sowers, R.N. for Public Health Nurse services to fill contract requirement for Immunization, 
Family Planning, TB, STD and HIV grants with the Arizona Department of Health Services at the rate of 
$35.00 per hour; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
c. Navajo County Public Health Services District Infectious Disease Preparedness and Response 
Presentation: John Zimmerman  addressed the Board. Dr. Wade Kartchner  gave a presentation on the 
Infectious Disease Preparedness and Response within Navajo County. Supervisor Nez made a motion to 
return to regular session; motion s econded by Supervisor Whitesinger . Vote unanimous approving the 
motion.

<!-- page 3 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 11-10-2014 
    Page 3 of 4 
 
      SHERIFF/FINANCE: Consideration and possible approval of a letter of support to transfer ownership of 
Northeast Arizona Training Center (NATC) to Northland Pioneer College (NPC): Mary Springer and Chief James 
Molesa discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion  to approve a letter of support to transfer 
ownership of Northeast Arizona Training Center (NATC) to Northland Pioneer College (NPC); motion seconded 
by Supervisor Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
PUBLIC WORKS:  
a. Consideration and possible approval of the expenditure of an amount not to exceed $37,500 from District 
II and $37,500 from District I CIP Funds for the crushing and hauling of Red Dog Material : Bill Bess 
discussed the item. Supervisor Nez made a motion to approve the expenditure of an amount not to exceed 
$37,500 from District II and $37,500 from District I CIP Funds for the crushing and hauling of Red Dog 
Material; motion seconded by Supervisor Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. NAVAJO COUNTY FLOOD CONTROL DISTRICT Board of Directors Session : Supervisor Nez 
made a motion to enter into the Navajo County Flood Control District  Board of Directors Session ; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Consideration and 
possible approval of Amendment Number 1, extending the contract with the Tomhave Group, Inc. an 
additional one year term for Legislative Advocacy, Request for Proposal Number F13-10-15: Bill Bess 
discussed the item. Supervisor Nez made a motion to approve the Amendment Number 1, extending the 
contract with the Tomhave Group, Inc. an additional one year term for Legislative Advocacy, Request for 
Proposal Number F13-10-15; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger.  Vote unanimous approving 
the motion. Supervisor Nez made a motion to return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
BOARD OF EQUALIZATION : Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion  to enter into the Board of 
Equalization session; motion seconded by Supervisor Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
a. CONSENT AGENDA : Approval of agreement made by petitioners and County Assessor regarding 
Petition for Review of Notice of Change: Supervisor Nez made a motion to authorize the Chairman to 
sign the items in the consent agenda as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote 
unanimous approving the motion. 
b. Discussion and possible action regarding Petition for  Review of Taxpayer Notice of Change APN 206-
11-276A; Swearing in of Assessor, Appraisers and Petitioners : Assessor Cammy Darris discussed FCV 
$105,305.00 with a LCV of $105,301.00 and stated that in accordance with Arizona Revised Statute 42-
16-106 the petitioner shall appear in person to dispute property values.  Petitioner is not present. Assessor 
Cammy Darris is asking the board to uphold t he Assessor’s evaluation. Supervisor Nez made a motion 
to uphold the Assessor’s property evaluation as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. 
Vote unanimous approving the motion. Supervisor Nez made a motion  to return to regular session; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE: Report from Board members , County Attorney and County Manager : 
Supervisor Whiting gave a report from District III; he indicated that with the recent election residents are asking 
if he will serve as Mayor of Snowflake.  Supervisor Whiting was on the ballot but had turned in a notice declining 
appointment after accepting appointment to the Board of Supervisor, District III seat. The Snowflake council will 
meet tomorrow and we will appoint a Mayor who will serve for a two year term.  Supervisor Whiting discussed 
attending a radio interview Supervisor Tenney hosted in Show Low and discussed some of the things regarding 
“We are Navajo C ounty”. He also accompanied Ms. Young to the Snowflake Chamber of Commerce  meeting 
where she is showing the “We are Navajo County” presentation.  Supervisor Whiting commented that he has 
had a chance to meet with staff , Mr. Clark & Mr. Menlove  regarding White Mountain Recreation District and

<!-- page 4 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 11-10-2014 
    Page 4 of 4 
 
some of their concerns.  Also he indicated that he has met with Mr. Moore, Mr. Jayne and the AZ Land Department 
on the future of potash as it relates to NACOG and their projects within Navajo County. Supervisor Nez reported 
from District I. Supervisor Nez said Happy Birthday to James Jayne and Melissa Buckley. He reported on the 
Navajo Nation election . He spoke about the Navajo Supreme Court ruling on the matter of Mr. Descheenie’s 
removal from the ballot to be heard by the OHA, Office of Hearings and Appeals, within 5 days.   He reported on 
Mr. Begaye’s request for him to be his Vice Presidential running mate and he has accepted the invitation after 
discussions with family and prayer. A special election will be held on December 23. If successful he will have to 
step down from his District I, Board of Supervisor seat in January 2015. Supervisor Whitesinger reported on 
District V. She thanked Supervisor Nez for his leadership and values all the things that she has learned from him. 
She reported on a Board meeting she attended for conservation legacy and stated that we are fortunate to have a 
White Mountain Youth core group here in Navajo County  which works under the Arizona Conservation Co re.  
One of the purposes of the group is not only to teach our young to be better stewards with the environment but to 
also be better stewards of themselves .  She feels strongly about our young people will be the ones who will be 
making future changes that will be needed in our communities. She indicated that she serves on First Things First 
White Mountain Regional Partnership Council and at their last meeting their discussion was in support of an early 
childhood facility in Cibeque.  We need to educate on the importance of early development to provide our young 
people with the skill sets they require to transition into elementary schools. Supervisor Whitesinger spoke about 
Sunrise Ski Resort receiving an Excellence in Leadership Award. She expressed thanks to our Veterans for their 
service. Mr. Jayne congratulated Supervisor Whitesinger for her work with the Sunrise Ski Resort Board and 
commented on the huge turnout for the awards ceremony .  He reported on the support letter for NPC and the 
NATC building. He spoke of the Local law enforcement academy  and thanked Chief Molesa for allowing our 
community to attend an academy locally and not have to travel to larger cities for training.  Mr. Jayne thanked 
Sheriff Clark for his leadership on this initiative. Supervisor Whitesinger clarified that she will be transitioning 
to Board Chair of Sunrise Sky Resort in the next month. Chairman Thompson reported on District II. He spoke 
of the importance of getting individuals involved in voting. He also indicated that he has been visiting with various 
organizations for road improvements. Chairman Thompson  congratulated Supervisor Nez  for his political 
commitment. He also spoke of our veterans again thanking them for their service. Chairman Thompson  
indicated there would be a Progressive Thanksgiving Luncheon following the Board of Supervisors meeting in 
the Code Talkers Conference room and in the Cafeteria. 
ADJOURN: At 10:59  a.m. Supervisor Nez made a motion to adjourn ; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
APPROVED:       DATED: November 25, 2014 
 
Jesse Thompson, Chairman 
Navajo County Board of Supervisors 
 
ATTEST: 
 
Melissa W. Buckley, Clerk 
Navajo County Board of Supervisor
